# Intergenerational persistence by the child's age at measurement, by race

Generated by `26.IGEByAge.py` (2026-10-17). Every cell: coefficient (s.e.; p-value), *** p<0.01, ** p<0.05, * p<0.1; s.e. clustered by 1968 family (NLSY79: 1979 household), joint across bands (stacked, fully interacted), normal p-values. E[rank] p-values against 50. Race = parental household head's race. Black / White log IGE unweighted (as `6.IGEMain.py`); Pooled = all races, weighted (child-year core longitudinal weight); ranks always weighted (wt_pt, `7.RankMobility.py`). White - Black = difference of the two race regressions. N row: child-years (or child-bands) / children / clusters for Black (B) and White (W). Age = age at interview.

## 2. Tests: does the IGE, and the Black-White gap in it, change with age? (PSID, child-year design)

Rows per panel: Wald test that the slope (or the White - Black gap) is equal in all bands; linear trend of it in the band midpoint per 10 years; mean in bands < 35, mean in bands >= 35 and their difference. The 31-37 window is excluded.

## 5. Interpretation checks (PSID)

### 5.1 Haider-Solon: income growth by parental income (fixed children observed in both bands)

One regression per column: (1) mean ln y in the early band, (2) in the late band, (3) late - early (controls: A quartic; (3) also the change in mean age). (3) is the change of the IGE with age in a fixed sample; > 0 means children of richer parents have steeper growth.

### 5.4 Log IGE vs rank slope on the same children and weights (PSID, family income)

Rank frame of section 4 (parents 12-17, child band mean >= 2 years), children with positive band-mean income, weighted by wt_pt, no controls. Log IGE = Corr(logs) x SD ratio (logs); rank slope = Corr(ranks) x SD ratio (ranks; national ranks within birth year and band). Hybrids: ln child income on parent rank / 100 and child rank on ln parental income. Bootstrap s.e. (200 draws of 1968 families, ranks fixed); p-values against 0 (correlation / SD-ratio p-values are therefore trivial).

## 6. NLSY79 (cohorts 1960-64): log IGE and rank mobility by age band

Parents: mean family income 1978-80 rounds; controls: quartic in (parent's age - 45)/10 and single-year age dummies; Pooled = Black + White weighted by wt79 (the NLSY79 file holds Black and non-Black non-Hispanic respondents only). Ranks as in section 4.
